\displaystyle\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}=-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{cov}^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y),{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}\}

is negative definite.
Hence, it suffices to show the existence of a local maximizer inside a ball
centered at
{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}
with radius of order
(nh)^{-1/2}
.
We will show this by proving for any
\epsilon>0
, there exists a constant
C>0
such that

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}\left[l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})>\sup_{\|{\bf v}\|_{2}=C}l\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}+(nh)^{-1/2}{\bf v}\}\right]\geq 1-2\epsilon

(S.14)

for sufficiently large
n
.

Let
{\bf v}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
be an arbitrary vector with
\|{\bf v}\|_{2}=C
and

{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}\equiv\alpha\{{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}+(nh)^{-1/2}{\bf v}\}+(1-\alpha){\bm{\gamma}}_{0}
for some
\alpha\in(0,1)
.
By the Taylor expansion, we have

\displaystyle l\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}+(nh)^{-1/2}{\bf v}\}-l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})=(nh)^{-1/2}\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}{\bf v}+\frac{1}{2}(nh)^{-1}{\bf v}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}{\bf v}.

(S.15)

We first analyze
\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}
.
Let
{\bf u}\in U
where
U\equiv\{{\bf u}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}:\|{\bf u}\|_{2}=1\}
, then

\displaystyle n^{-1}\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}{\bf u}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[{\bf B}(y_{i})^{\rm T}{\bf u}-E^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\}]

(S.16)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[{\bf B}(y_{i})^{\rm T}{\bf u}-E\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}_{i}\}]

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[E\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}_{i}\}-E^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\}].

The first term of (
S.16
) satisfies

\displaystyle E\left\{\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[{\bf B}(Y_{i})^{\rm T}{\bf u}-E\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf X}_{i}\}\right]\right)^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}E\left(\left[{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}-E\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf X}\}\right]^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}E[\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf X}\}].

Furthermore, by Lemma
3
we have
\sup_{{\bf u}\in U}E[\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf X}\}]\asymp h
,
which implies

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i}\}]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf u}\in U}\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[{\bf B}(y_{i})^{\rm T}{\bf u}-E\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}_{i}\}]\right|

(S.17)

\displaystyle\asymp_{p}

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}h^{1/2}.

Now, the second term of (
S.16
) satisfies

\displaystyle\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[E\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}_{i}\}-E^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\}]\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sup_{{\bf x}\in\mathcal{X}}|E\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}\}-E^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\}|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(h^{q})\|{\bf B}(\cdot)^{\rm T}{\bf u}\|_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(h^{q+1/2})

by Lemma
1
and
4
. Hence, we get

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i}\}-E^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\}]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(h^{q+1/2})

(S.18)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(n^{-1/2}h^{1/2})

under Conditions
(C3)
and
(C4)
.
Therefore, by (
S.16
), (
S.17
) and (
S.18
), we get

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}\right\|_{2}\asymp_{p}(nh)^{1/2},

hence for any
\epsilon>0
, there exists a constant
0<C_{1}<\infty
such that

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}\left\{\left\|\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}\right\|_{2}\leq C_{1}(nh)^{1/2}\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon.

(S.19)

Next, to analyze
\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}
, we have

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{\rm T}\left\{-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right\}{\bf u}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}\}

(S.20)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[\hbox{var}^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}\}-\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}_{i}\}]

\displaystyle+\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}_{i}\}-E[\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf X}\}]\right)

\displaystyle+E[\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf X}\}].

Note that
\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}=o(1)
by construction.
Then by Lemma
1
and
5
, the first term satisfies

\displaystyle\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[\hbox{var}^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}\}-\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}_{i}\}]\right|

(S.21)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sup_{{\bf x}\in\mathcal{X}}|\hbox{var}^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}\}-\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}\}|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1)\left[\|\{{\bf B}(\cdot)^{\rm T}{\bf u}\}^{2}\|_{1}+\|{\bf B}(\cdot)^{\rm T}{\bf u}\|_{1}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1)\left\{\|{\bf B}(\cdot)^{\rm T}{\bf u}\|_{2}^{2}+\|{\bf B}(\cdot)^{\rm T}{\bf u}\|_{1}^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(h).

For the second term of (
S.20
), first let
w_{i}\equiv\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}_{i}\}
.
Note that by Lemma
3
,
there exists a constant
C
so that
0\leq w_{i}\leq Ch
and
0\leq E(W_{i})\leq Ch
for all
i=1,\dots,n
.
Therefore, there exists a constant
C_{w}
, where
0<C_{w}<\infty
,
such that
|w_{i}-E(W_{i})|\leq C_{w}h
for all
i=1,\dots,n
.
This also directly implies
\hbox{var}(W_{i})\leq C_{w}^{2}h^{2}
.
Then, by the Bernstein’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}\left\{n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}-E(W_{i})\geq\epsilon\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp\left\{-\frac{n^{2}\epsilon^{2}/2}{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}(W_{i})+C_{w}hn\epsilon/3}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\exp\left\{-\frac{n^{2}\epsilon^{2}/2}{nC_{w}^{2}h^{2}+C_{w}hn\epsilon/3}\right\}

\displaystyle\to

\displaystyle 0

for
\epsilon=h(\hbox{log}n)^{-1}
as
n\to\infty
. Note that
\epsilon=o(h)
. Thus, we get

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}w_{i}-E(W_{i})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}_{i}\}-E[\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf X}\}]

(S.22)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(h).

Now for the last term of (
S.20
), we have

\displaystyle\|{\bf\Sigma}_{22}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf u}\in U}{\bf u}^{\rm T}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}{\bf u}

(S.23)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf u}\in U}E[\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf X}\}]

\displaystyle\asymp

\displaystyle h

by Lemma
3
.
Hence Condition
(C6)
implies that
{\bf\Sigma}_{22}
has all eigenvalues of order
h
.
Moreover, from (
S.20
), (
S.21
), (
S.22
) and (
S.23
), we have

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{\rm T}\left\{-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right\}{\bf u}={\bf u}^{\rm T}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}{\bf u}+o_{p}(h)

(S.24)

uniformly for all
{\bf u}
.
Thus, all eigenvalues of
\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}
are negative and of order
nh

with probability approaching 1, which implies there exists a constant
0<C_{2}<\infty
such that

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}\left\{{\bf v}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}{\bf v}\leq-C^{2}C_{2}nh\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon.

(S.25)

Combining (
S.15
), (
S.19
), and (
S.25
), we get

\displaystyle l\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}+(nh)^{-1/2}{\bf v}\}-l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(nh)^{-1/2}C_{1}(nh)^{1/2}+\frac{1}{2}(nh)^{-1}(-C^{2}C_{2}nh)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C\left(C_{1}-\frac{C_{2}}{2}C\right)

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle 0

with probability at least
1-2\epsilon
when
C>2C_{1}/C_{2}
.
This proves (
S.14
), hence we have
\|\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}
.

Now we analyze the asymptotic behavior of
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})
.
Note that
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})
maximizes
l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}})
,
then by using the Taylor expansion with
{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}
on the line connecting
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})
and
{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}
,
we get

\displaystyle{\bf 0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial l\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}+\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\{\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\}.

This leads to

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right\}^{-1}n^{-1}\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i}\}]+{\bf r}_{1},

where

\displaystyle{\bf r}_{1}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\left[\left\{-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right\}^{-1}-{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}\right]n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i}\}]

\displaystyle+\left\{-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right\}^{-1}\left(n^{-1}\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}-n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i}\}]\right).

Note that

\displaystyle\left\|\left\{-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right\}^{-1}-{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left\{-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right\}^{-1}\left\{{\bf\Sigma}_{22}+n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right\}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\left\{-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right\}^{-1}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\bf\Sigma}_{22}+n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\asymp_{p}

\displaystyle h^{-1}\times o_{p}(h)\times h^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(h^{-1}),

by (
S.23
), (
S.24
) and Condition
(C6)
.
Furthermore, by (
S.16
) and (
S.18
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}-n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i}\}]\right\|_{2}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2}h^{1/2}).

Hence
{\bf r}_{1}
satisfies

\displaystyle\|{\bf r}_{1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{p}(h^{-1})n^{-1/2}h^{1/2}+h^{-1}o_{p}(n^{-1/2}h^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}

by (
S.17
) and (
S.24
).
Then by Condition
(C5)
, this leads to

\displaystyle\sup_{y\in[0,1]}|\widehat{c}(y,{\bm{\beta}}_{0})-c(y)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{y\in[0,1]}|{\bf B}(y)^{\rm T}\{\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\}|+\sup_{y\in[0,1]}|{\bf B}(y)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}-c(y)|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{y\in[0,1]}\|{\bf B}(y)\|_{2}\left(\|{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}\|_{2}\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i}\}]\right\|_{2}+\|{\bf r}_{1}\|_{2}\right)+O(h^{q})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(1)O(h^{-1})O_{p}(n^{-1/2}h^{1/2})+o_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}+O(h^{q})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}+h^{q}\}.

∎

S.5
Proof of Proposition
2

First we will show for any
\epsilon>0
, there exists a constant
C>0
such that

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}\left[l\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}>\sup_{\|{\bf v}\|_{2}=C}l\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0}+n^{-1/2}{\bf v},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0}+n^{-1/2}{\bf v})\}\right]\geq 1-3\epsilon

(S.26)

for a sufficiently large
n
.
This implies there exists a local maximizer of
l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}
,
say
\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}
, such that
\|\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2})
.
We will further show the Hessian of
l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}
is negative definite for any
{\bm{\beta}}
,
hence
\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}
is the global maximizer of
l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}
,
i.e.
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}=\widetilde{\bm{\beta}}
, and
hence
\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2})
.

By the Taylor expansion,

\displaystyle l\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0}+n^{-1/2}{\bf v},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0}+n^{-1/2}{\bf v})\}-l\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}

(S.27)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\frac{dl\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}}{d{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}{\bf v}+\frac{1}{2}n^{-1}{\bf v}^{\rm T}\frac{d^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{d{\bm{\beta}}d{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}{\bf v},

where
{\bm{\beta}}^{*}\equiv\alpha_{1}({\bm{\beta}}_{0}+n^{-1/2}{\bf v})+(1-\alpha_{1}){\bm{\beta}}_{0}
for
some
\alpha_{1}\in(0,1)
. Noting that

\partial l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}={\bf 0}
for any
{\bm{\beta}}
, we get

\displaystyle\frac{dl\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}}{d{\bm{\beta}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial l\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}+\frac{\partial\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}\frac{\partial l\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial l\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}+\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\{\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\},

where
{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}=\alpha_{2}\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})+(1-\alpha_{2}){\bm{\gamma}}_{0}
for some

\alpha_{2}\in(0,1)
. In addition,

\displaystyle\frac{d^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{d{\bm{\beta}}d{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}\left[\frac{\partial l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}\right]\biggr|_{{\bm{\beta}}={\bm{\beta}}^{*}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}+\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\frac{\partial\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}.

Now since
\partial l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}={\bf 0}
for any
{\bm{\beta}}
,

\displaystyle{\bf 0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}\left[\frac{\partial l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}+\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\frac{\partial\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}.

Note that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}=-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{cov}^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y),{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}}\}

is negative definite. Then,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}=-\left[\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right]^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}.

(S.28)

Hence, we can write (
S.27
) as

\displaystyle l\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0}+n^{-1/2}{\bf v},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0}+n^{-1/2}{\bf v})\}-l\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}

(S.29)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\left[\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}+\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\{\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\}\right]^{\rm T}{\bf v}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}{\bf v}^{\rm T}n^{-1}\Bigg(\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}-\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right]^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}\Bigg){\bf v}.

In (
S.29
), we first analyze
\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})/\partial{\bm{\beta}}
. We have

\displaystyle n^{-1}\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E^{*}(Y|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y|{\bf x}_{i})\}+n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\{E(Y|{\bf x}_{i})-E^{*}(Y|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})\}.

By Conditions
(C1)
and
(C7)
, the first term satisfies

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y|{\bf x}_{i})\}\right\|_{2}\asymp_{p}n^{-1/2}.

For the second term, by Lemma
4
we get

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\{E(Y|{\bf x}_{i})-E^{*}(Y|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})\}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|{\bf x}_{i}\right\|_{2}\sup_{{\bf x}\in\mathcal{X}}|E(Y|{\bf x})-E^{*}(Y|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(h^{q}),

where the last equality holds because
E(\|{\bf X}\|_{2})
is bounded by Condition
(C1)
.
Note that
h^{q}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2})
under Conditions
(C3)
and
(C4)
.
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}\right\|_{2}\asymp_{p}n^{1/2},

and

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}-\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y|{\bf x}_{i})\}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}(n^{1/2}).

(S.30)

Moreover, for any
\epsilon>0
, there exists a constant
0<C_{1}<\infty
such that

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}\left\{\left\|\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}\right\|_{2}\leq C_{1}n^{1/2}\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon.

(S.31)

Now we analyze
\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})/\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}
.
Let
{\bf u}_{m}\in U_{m}\equiv\{{\bf u}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}:\|{\bf u}\|_{2}=1\}
, then for fixed
{\bm{\beta}}
and
{\bm{\gamma}}
we have

\displaystyle\left\|-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}{\bf u}_{m}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\hbox{cov}^{*}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}_{m}|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}}\}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\|{\bf x}_{i}\right\|_{2}\sup_{{\bf x}\in\mathcal{X}}|\hbox{cov}^{*}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}_{m}|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}}\}|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(h^{1/2})

by Condition
(C1)
and Lemma
2
.
Then using the fact that
U_{m}
is compact, taking supremum with respect to
{\bf u}_{m}
gives

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(nh^{1/2}).

(S.32)

Further recall that
\|\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}
by Proposition
1
.
This implies

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\{\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(n^{1/2}),

and equivalently, there exists a constant
0<C_{2}<\infty
such that

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}\left\{\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\{\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\}\right\|_{2}\leq C_{2}n^{1/2}\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon.

(S.33)

We next show
-n^{-1}\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})/\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}
converges to
{\bf\Sigma}_{12}
.
Note that
\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}=o_{p}(1)
.
Then by Condition
(C1)
, Lemma
1
and
5
, we get

\displaystyle\left\|\left(-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}-{\bf\Sigma}_{12}\right){\bf u}_{m}\right\|_{2}

(S.34)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}[\hbox{cov}^{*}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}_{m}|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}\}-\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}_{m}|{\bf x}_{i}\}]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}_{m}|{\bf x}_{i}\}-E[{\bf X}\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}_{m}|{\bf X}\}]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\|{\bf x}_{i}\|_{2}\right)o_{p}(h^{1/2})+o_{p}(n^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(h^{1/2}).

The last equality holds because
n^{-1/2}=o(h^{1/2})
by Conditions
(C3)
and
(C4)
.
This implies

\displaystyle\left\|-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}-{\bf\Sigma}_{12}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}(h^{1/2}).

(S.35)

Now we show
-n^{-1}\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}/\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}
converges to
{\bf\Sigma}_{11}
.
First we will show that

\|\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}
.
Note that since

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}/\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}&\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}/\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}\\
\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}&\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}{\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}\hbox{var}^{*}\{Y|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}&{\bf x}_{i}\hbox{cov}^{*}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}\\
[{\bf x}_{i}\hbox{cov}^{*}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}]^{\rm T}&\hbox{cov}^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y),{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{cov}^{*}\left\{\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}Y\\
{\bf B}(Y)\end{bmatrix},\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}Y\\
{\bf B}(Y)\end{bmatrix}\Bigg|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\right\}

is negative definite,
\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}/\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}

and
\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}
are negative definite,
and the Schur complement of
\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}
,

\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}-\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right]^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}},

is also negative definite. Let
{\bf u}_{p}\in U_{p}\equiv\{{\bf u}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}:\|{\bf u}\|_{2}=1\}
, then we have

\displaystyle 0\leq{\bf u}_{p}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\left[-\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right]^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}{\bf u}_{p}<{\bf u}_{p}^{\rm T}\left[-\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}\right]{\bf u}_{p}.

It is easy to see that

\displaystyle{\bf u}_{p}^{\rm T}\left[-\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}\right]{\bf u}_{p}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bf u}_{p})^{2}\hbox{var}^{*}\{Y|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}\asymp_{p}n,

and

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf u}_{m}\in U_{m}}{\bf u}_{m}^{\rm T}\left[-\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right]{\bf u}_{m}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf u}_{m}\in U_{m}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}_{m}|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}

\displaystyle\asymp_{p}

\displaystyle nh

by Lemma
3
. Hence we have

\displaystyle\left\|\left[\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right]^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}\right\|_{2}=O_{p}(h^{-1/2}).

(S.36)

Now, (
S.28
) and the Taylor expansion of
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})
with

{\bm{\beta}}^{**}
on the line connecting
{\bm{\beta}}_{0}
and
{\bm{\beta}}^{*}
gives

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}-\left[\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{**},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{**})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right]^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{**},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{**})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}).

Recall that
\|\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}
by Proposition
1

and
\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}=O(n^{-1/2})
by construction.
These imply
\|\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}
.
Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\left|{\bf u}_{p}^{\rm T}\left[-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}-{\bf\Sigma}_{11}\right]{\bf u}_{p}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bf u}_{p})^{2}[\hbox{var}^{*}\{Y|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}-\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf x}_{i})]\right|

\displaystyle+\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bf u}_{p})^{2}\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf x}_{i})-E\{({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bf u}_{p})^{2}\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf X})\}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1)

by Condition
(C1)
and Lemma
5
, which implies

\displaystyle\left\|-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}-{\bf\Sigma}_{11}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}(1).

(S.37)

Below, we show
-n^{-1}d^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}/d{\bm{\beta}}d{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}
converges to
{\bf\Sigma}^{*}
.
Using similar argument to (
S.34
), we get

\displaystyle\left\|-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}-{\bf\Sigma}_{12}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}(h^{1/2}).

(S.38)

In addition, by the similar proof
to showing
-n^{-1}\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}

converges to
{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}
in Proposition
1
, we get

\displaystyle\left\|\left[-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right]^{-1}-{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}(h^{-1}).

(S.39)

Now, note that

\displaystyle\frac{d^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{d{\bm{\beta}}d{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}=\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}-\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\left[\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right]^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}.

Combining (
S.37
), (
S.38
), and (
S.39
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|-n^{-1}\frac{d^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{d{\bm{\beta}}d{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}-{\bf\Sigma}^{*}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}(1),

(S.40)

where
{\bf\Sigma}^{*}
is given in Condition
(C7)
.
This shows the Hessian at
{\bm{\beta}}^{*}
is negative definite
and its 2-norm is of order
n
with probability approaching 1.
Hence, there exists a constant
0<C_{3}<\infty
such that

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}\left\{{\bf v}^{\rm T}\frac{d^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{d{\bm{\beta}}d{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}{\bf v}\leq-C^{2}C_{3}n\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon.

(S.41)

Combining (
S.29
), (
S.31
), (
S.33
), and (
S.41
),
we have

\displaystyle l\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0}+n^{-1/2}{\bf v},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0}+n^{-1/2}{\bf v})\}-l\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Cn^{-1/2}(C_{1}n^{1/2}+C_{2}n^{1/2})+\frac{1}{2}n^{-1}(-C^{2}C_{3}n)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C\left\{(C_{1}+C_{2})-\frac{C_{3}}{2}C\right\}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle 0

with probability at least
1-3\epsilon
when
C>2(C_{1}+C_{2})/C_{3}
, which
proves (
S.26
).

We next show that the
Hessian of
l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}
is negative definite for all
{\bm{\beta}}
,
hence the local maximizer inside the disk with radius
Cn^{-1/2}
is
in fact the maximizer
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
, i.e.

\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2})
. We have

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}/\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}&\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}/\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}\\
\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}&\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}{\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}\hbox{var}^{*}\{Y|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}&{\bf x}_{i}\hbox{cov}^{*}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}\\
[{\bf x}_{i}\hbox{cov}^{*}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}]^{\rm T}&\hbox{cov}^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y),{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{cov}^{*}\left\{\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}Y\\
{\bf B}(Y)\end{bmatrix},\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}Y\\
{\bf B}(Y)\end{bmatrix}\Bigg|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\right\}

is negative definite at all
{\bm{\beta}}
.
Then since the Hessian is the Schur complement in the above matrix,
it is also negative definite for all
{\bm{\beta}}
.
Hence we conclude that
\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2})
.

Now, we analyze the asymptotic properties of
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
.
Since
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
maximizes
l\{{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}})\}
, we have

\displaystyle{\bf 0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{dl\{\widehat{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\}}{d{\bm{\beta}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{dl\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}}{d{\bm{\beta}}}+\frac{d^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{d{\bm{\beta}}d{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}),

by the Taylor expansion with
{\bm{\beta}}^{*}\equiv\alpha_{1}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}+(1-\alpha_{1}){\bm{\beta}}_{0}
for some
\alpha_{1}\in(0,1)
.
This implies

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}=\left[-n^{-1}\frac{d^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{d{\bm{\beta}}d{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}\right]^{-1}n^{-1}\frac{dl\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}}{d{\bm{\beta}}}.

Recall that for
{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}=\alpha_{2}\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})+(1-\alpha_{2}){\bm{\gamma}}_{0}
for some
\alpha_{2}\in(0,1)
,

\displaystyle\frac{dl\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}}{d{\bm{\beta}}}=\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}+\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\{\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\}.

Then by (
S.30
), (
S.32
), (
S.35
) and
Proposition
1
, we can write the gradient as

\displaystyle\frac{dl\{{\bm{\beta}}_{0},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}}{d{\bm{\beta}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y|{\bf x}_{i})\}

\displaystyle-{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i}\}]+{\bf r}_{2}^{\prime},

where
\|{\bf r}_{2}^{\prime}\|_{2}=o_{p}(n^{1/2})
.
Hence by (
S.40
),

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf\Sigma}^{*-1}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y|{\bf x}_{i})\}

\displaystyle-{\bf\Sigma}^{*-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i}\}]+{\bf r}_{2},

where
\|{\bf r}_{2}\|_{2}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2})
.
Thus by the central limit theorem and the Slutsky’s theorem, we get

\displaystyle{{\bf\Sigma}^{*}}^{1/2}\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\to N({\bf 0},{\bf I})

in distribution when
n\to\infty
.
∎

S.6
Lemmas

We introduce another lemmas for asymptotic properties of the profile estimators.

Lemma 6
.

Under Conditions
(C1)
-
(C7)
,
\|\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}
and

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}^{*-1}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y|{\bf x}_{i})\}

\displaystyle+({\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}+{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}^{*-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1})n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i}\}]+{\bf r}_{3},

where
\|{\bf r}_{3}\|_{2}=o_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}
.

Proof.

Based on (
S.28
) and the Taylor expansion of
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})
,
letting
{\bm{\beta}}^{*}
be on the line connecting
{\bm{\beta}}_{0}
and
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
,
we get

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}-\left[\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right]^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}).

(S.42)

It is easy to verify that
\|\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}

because
\|\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}_{0})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}
by Proposition
1
,

\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2})
by Proposition
2
and (
S.36
).
Now recall that
\|{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}\|_{2}\asymp h^{-1}
by (
S.23
) and Condition
(C6)
.
In addition, for any
{\bf u}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
such that
\|{\bf u}\|_{2}=1
, we get

\displaystyle\|{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf u}\|_{2}\leq\|E({\bf X})\|_{2}\sup_{{\bf x}\in\mathcal{X}}|\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}\}|=O(h^{1/2})

by Condition
(C1)
and Lemma
2
, which implies
\|{\bf\Sigma}_{21}\|_{2}=O(h^{1/2})
.
Then by (
S.38
) and (
S.39
), we get

\displaystyle{\bf R}\equiv-\left[\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right]^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}+{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}

satisfies
\|{\bf R}\|_{2}=o_{p}(h^{-1/2})
. Then by Proposition
2
, we have

\displaystyle-\left[\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right]^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}^{*-1}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y|{\bf x}_{i})\}

\displaystyle+{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}^{*-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i}\}]+{\bf r}_{3}^{\prime},

where

\displaystyle\|{\bf r}_{3}^{\prime}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|{\bf R}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}-{\bf r}_{2})-\left[\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right]^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}{\bf r}_{2}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|{\bf R}\|_{2}(\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}+\|{\bf r}_{2}\|_{2})+\left\|\left[\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\right]^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l\{{\bm{\beta}}^{*},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*})\}}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}\right\|_{2}\|{\bf r}_{2}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{p}(h^{-1/2})O_{p}(n^{-1/2})+O_{p}(h^{-1/2})o_{p}(n^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}

by Proposition
2
and (
S.36
).
Combining this with Proposition
1
and (
S.42
), we get

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}^{*-1}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y|{\bf x}_{i})\}

\displaystyle+({\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}+{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}^{*-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1})n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i}\}]+{\bf r}_{3},

where
\|{\bf r}_{3}\|_{2}=o_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}
.
∎

Lemma 7
.

Under Conditions
(C1)
-
(C7)
,

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\\
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\end{bmatrix}={\bf\Sigma}^{-1}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y|{\bf x}_{i})\}\\
{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i}\}\end{bmatrix}+\begin{bmatrix}{\bf r}_{2}\\
{\bf r}_{3}\end{bmatrix},

where
\|{\bf r}_{2}\|_{2}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2})
and
\|{\bf r}_{3}\|_{2}=o_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}
.

Proof.

Note that Proposition
2
and Lemma
6
lead to

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\\
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}{\bf C}_{11}&{\bf C}_{12}\\
{\bf C}_{21}&{\bf C}_{22}\end{bmatrix}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y|{\bf x}_{i})\}\\
{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i}\}\end{bmatrix}+\begin{bmatrix}{\bf r}_{2}\\
{\bf r}_{3}\end{bmatrix},

where
\|{\bf r}_{2}\|_{2}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2})
,
\|{\bf r}_{3}\|_{2}=o_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}
, and

\displaystyle{\bf C}_{11}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle{\bf\Sigma}^{*-1},

\displaystyle{\bf C}_{12}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle-{\bf\Sigma}^{*-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}={\bf C}_{21}^{\rm T},

\displaystyle{\bf C}_{22}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}+{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}^{*-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}.

In addition, it is easy to see that

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}{\bf C}_{11}&{\bf C}_{12}\\
{\bf C}_{21}&{\bf C}_{22}\end{bmatrix}={\bf\Sigma}^{-1}.

∎

S.7
Proof of Theorem
1

We can write
\widehat{\bm{\xi}}-{\bm{\xi}}_{0}
as

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\xi}}-{\bm{\xi}}_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\,n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}^{*}\{Y|{\bf x}_{i},\widehat{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\}

(S.43)

\displaystyle+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\,n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[\hbox{var}^{*}\{Y|{\bf x}_{i},\widehat{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\}-\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf x}_{i})\right]

\displaystyle+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\left[n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf x}_{i})-E\{\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf X})\}\right].

Noting that
\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2})
by Proposition
2

and
\|\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}
by Lemma
6
,
it is easy to see that

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}^{*}\{Y|{\bf x}_{i},\widehat{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[\hbox{var}^{*}\{Y|{\bf x}_{i},\widehat{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\}-\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf x}_{i})]

(S.44)

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf x}_{i})-E\{\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf X})\}]+E\{\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf X})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\{\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf X})\}+o_{p}(1)

by Lemma
5
. Next, we have

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[\hbox{var}^{*}\{Y|{\bf x}_{i},\widehat{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\}-\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf x}_{i})\right]

(S.45)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[\hbox{var}^{*}\{Y|{\bf x}_{i},\widehat{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\}-\hbox{var}^{*}(Y|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})+\hbox{var}^{*}(Y|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})-\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf x}_{i})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[\frac{\partial\hbox{var}^{*}(Y|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})+\frac{\partial\hbox{var}^{*}(Y|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\{\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\}\right]+O(h^{q}),

where
({\bm{\beta}}^{*\rm T},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*\rm T})^{\rm T}
is a point on the line
connecting
\{\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})^{\rm T}\}^{\rm T}
and
({\bm{\beta}}_{0}^{\rm T},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}^{\rm T})^{\rm T}
.
The last equality holds by Lemma
4
.
Then by Lemma
5
, it is easy to check that

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\partial\hbox{var}^{*}(Y|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}-E[\{Y-E(Y|{\bf X})\}^{3}{\bf X}]\right\|_{2}

(S.46)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}E^{*}[\{Y-E^{*}(Y|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}^{3}|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}]{\bf x}_{i}-E[\{Y-E(Y|{\bf X})\}^{3}{\bf X}]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1).

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\hbox{var}^{*}(Y|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}=E^{*}(\{Y-E^{*}(Y|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})\}^{2}[{\bf B}(Y)-E^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}}\}]|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}}).

(S.47)

Note that for
{\bf u}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
such that
\|{\bf u}\|_{2}=1
,

\displaystyle E^{*}[\{Y-E^{*}(Y|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})\}^{2}{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}}]^{\rm T}{\bf u}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|{\bf B}(\cdot)^{\rm T}{\bf u}\|_{1}\sup_{y\in[0,1],{\bf x}\in\mathcal{X}}\{y-E^{*}(Y|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})\}^{2}f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}^{*}(y,{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(h^{1/2})

by Condition
(C1)
and Lemma
1
. Similar argument will show

\displaystyle E^{*}[\{Y-E^{*}(Y|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})\}^{2}E^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}}\}|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}}]^{\rm T}{\bf u}=O(h^{1/2}),

and

\displaystyle E(\{Y-E(Y|{\bf x})\}^{2}[{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}\}]|{\bf x})^{\rm T}{\bf u}=O(h^{1/2}).

(S.48)

Then (
S.47
), Condition
(C1)
, and Lemma
5
lead to

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf x}\in\mathcal{X}}\left|\frac{\partial\hbox{var}^{*}(Y|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}{\bf u}-E(\{Y-E(Y|{\bf x})\}^{2}[{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}\}]|{\bf x})^{\rm T}{\bf u}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(h^{1/2}).

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\partial\hbox{var}^{*}(Y|{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}-E(\{Y-E(Y|{\bf X})\}^{2}[{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf X}\}])\right\|_{2}=o_{p}(h^{1/2}).

Using the fact that
\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2})
and
\|\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}
,
we combine the above with (
S.45
) and (
S.46
) so get

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[\hbox{var}^{*}\{Y|{\bf x}_{i},\widehat{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\}-\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf x}_{i})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E[\{Y-E(Y|{\bf X})\}^{3}{\bf X}]^{\rm T}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})

\displaystyle+E(\{Y-E(Y|{\bf X})\}^{2}[{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf X}\}])^{\rm T}\{\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\}+o_{p}(n^{-1/2}).

Then, we can rewrite (
S.43
) using (
S.44
) as

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\xi}}-{\bm{\xi}}_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(E\{\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf X})\}{\bf I}+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}E[\{Y-E(Y|{\bf X})\}^{3}{\bf X}^{\rm T}])(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})

(S.49)

\displaystyle+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}E(\{Y-E(Y|{\bf X})\}^{2}[{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf X}\}])^{\rm T}\{\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\}

\displaystyle+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf x}_{i})-E\{\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf X})\}\right]+{\bf r},

where
\|{\bf r}\|_{2}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2})
.
Hence we get
\|\widehat{\bm{\xi}}-{\bm{\xi}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2})
by (
S.48
).

Now we derive the asymptotic distribution of
\widehat{\bm{\xi}}
.
Recall from Theorem
1
that
{\bf A}=[{\bf A}_{1},{\bf A}_{2}]
and

\displaystyle{\bf A}_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\{\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf X})\}{\bf I}+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}E[\{Y-E(Y|{\bf X})\}^{3}{\bf X}^{\rm T}],

\displaystyle{\bf A}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}_{0}E(\{Y-E(Y|{\bf X})\}^{2}[{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf X}\}])^{\rm T}.

Then by Lemma
7
, (
S.49
) equals to

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\xi}}-{\bm{\xi}}_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf A}{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y|{\bf x}_{i})\}\\
{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i}\}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf x}_{i})-E\{\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf X})\}\right]

\displaystyle+{\bf A}_{1}{\bf r}_{2}+{\bf A}_{2}{\bf r}_{3}+{\bf r}.

We obviously have

\displaystyle\hbox{cov}[{\bf X}\{Y-E(Y|{\bf X})\},\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf X})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf 0}_{p},

\displaystyle\hbox{cov}[{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf X}\},\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf X})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf 0}_{m}.

In addition, recall that
\|{\bf r}_{2}\|_{2}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2})
and
\|{\bf r}_{3}\|_{2}=o_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}

by Lemma
7
,
and
\|{\bf A}_{2}\|_{2}=O(h^{1/2})
by (
S.48
),
then the remainders satisfy

\displaystyle\|{\bf A}_{1}{\bf r}_{2}+{\bf A}_{2}{\bf r}_{3}+{\bf r}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|{\bf A}_{1}\|_{2}\|{\bf r}_{2}\|_{2}+\|{\bf A}_{2}\|_{2}\|{\bf r}_{3}\|_{2}+\|{\bf r}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(1)o_{p}(n^{-1/2})+O(h^{1/2})o_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}+o_{p}(n^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(n^{-1/2}).

Hence, letting
{\bf\Sigma}_{\bm{\xi}}\equiv{\bf A}{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}{\bf A}^{\rm T}+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}{\bm{\beta}}_{0}^{\rm T}\hbox{var}\{\hbox{var}(Y|{\bf X})\}
,

{\bf\Sigma}_{\bm{\xi}}^{-1/2}\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\bm{\xi}}-{\bm{\xi}}_{0})
converges to the normal distribution
with mean
{\bf 0}
and variance
{\bf I}
.
∎

S.8
Proof of Theorem
2

For notational brevity, we denote
\nu\equiv{\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}
,
q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})
be such that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})}\exp\{t\nu+{\bf B}(t)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}dt=\tau\int_{0}^{1}\exp\{t\nu+{\bf B}(t)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}dt,

and

\displaystyle{q^{*}}^{\prime}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\frac{\partial q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})}{\partial\nu}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\tau\int_{0}^{1}t\exp\{t\nu+{\bf B}(t)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}dt-\int_{0}^{y}t\exp\{t\nu+{\bf B}(t)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}dt}{\exp\{y\nu+{\bf B}(y)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}}\bigg|_{y=q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{E^{*}([\tau-I\{Y<q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}]Y|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}^{*}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}}),{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}}\}}.

Let
{\bm{\beta}}^{*}
and
{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}
be such that
\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}=o_{p}(1)
and
\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=o_{p}(1)
respectively.
First note that

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf x}\in\mathcal{X}}|q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})-q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})|=o_{p}(1).

(S.50)

This is because

\displaystyle 0

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}^{*}(y,{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})dy-\int_{0}^{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})}f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}(y|{\bf x})dy

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}\{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}^{*}(y,{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})-f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}(y|{\bf x})\}dy+\int_{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})}^{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}(y|{\bf x})dy,

which implies, by Condition
(C1)
and Lemma
5
, that

\displaystyle c_{f}|q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})-q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}\{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}^{*}(y,{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})-f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}(y|{\bf x})\}dy\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1)

uniformly in
{\bf x}
, where
c_{f}=\inf_{y\in[0,1],{\bf x}\in\mathcal{X}}f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}(y|{\bf x})
.
Condition
(C1)
and Lemma
5
further lead to

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf x}\in\mathcal{X}}|{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})-q^{\prime}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})|=o_{p}(1),

(S.51)

since uniformly in
{\bf x},y
,

\displaystyle\left|f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}^{*}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}),{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}\}-f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}\{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})|{\bf x}\}\right|

(S.52)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}^{*}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}),{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}\}-f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})|{\bf x}\}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})|{\bf x}\}-f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}\{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})|{\bf x}\}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1),

|E^{*}(Y|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})-E(Y|{\bf x})|=o_{p}(1)
,
and for an arbitrary
g(\cdot)
such that
\|g(\cdot)\|_{2}<\infty
,

\displaystyle\left|E^{*}\left[I\{Y<q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}g(Y)|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}\right]-E\left[I\{Y<q({\bm{\beta}}_{0}^{\rm T}{\bf x})\}g(Y)|{\bf x}\right]\right|

(S.53)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}g(y)\{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}^{*}(y,{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})-f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}(y|{\bf x})\}dy\right|+\left|\int_{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})}^{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}g(y)f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}(y|{\bf x})dy\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{p}(1)\|g(\cdot)\|_{2}+\left|\int_{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})}^{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}\{g(y)\}^{2}dy\int_{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})}^{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}\{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}(y|{\bf x})\}^{2}dy\right|^{1/2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle o_{p}(1)\|g(\cdot)\|_{2}+C_{f}|q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})-q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})|^{1/2}\|g(\cdot)\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1)\|g(\cdot)\|_{2}.

Similarly, we also have

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf x}\in\mathcal{X}}|{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})-q^{\prime}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})|=O(h^{q})

(S.54)

by Condition
(C1)
and Lemma
4
.

In addition, we can show that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial{q^{*}}^{\prime}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}

(S.55)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\tau\int_{0}^{1}{\bf x}t^{2}\exp\{t\nu+{\bf B}(t)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}dt}{\exp[q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\nu+{\bf B}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}]}

\displaystyle-\frac{\int_{0}^{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})}{\bf x}t^{2}\exp\{t\nu+{\bf B}(t)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}dt+q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\exp[q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\nu+{\bf B}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}]{\bf x}{q^{*}}^{\prime}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})}{\exp[q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\nu+{\bf B}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}]}

\displaystyle-\frac{\tau\int_{0}^{1}t\exp\{t\nu+{\bf B}(t)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}dt-\int_{0}^{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})}t\exp\{t\nu+{\bf B}(t)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}dt}{\exp[q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\nu+{\bf B}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}]}

\displaystyle\times\left[{\bf x}{q^{*}}^{\prime}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\nu+q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}}){\bf x}+{\bf B}^{\prime}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}{\bf x}{q^{*}}^{\prime}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf x}\Bigg\{\frac{E^{*}([\tau-I\{Y\leq q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}]Y^{2}|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}^{*}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}}),{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}}\}}

\displaystyle-2{q^{*}}^{\prime}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})-\{{q^{*}}^{\prime}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}^{2}[\nu+{\bf B}^{\prime}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}]\Bigg\},

\displaystyle\frac{\partial q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\tau\int_{0}^{1}{\bf B}(t)\exp\{t\nu+{\bf B}(t)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}dt-\int_{0}^{y}{\bf B}(t)\exp\{t\nu+{\bf B}(t)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}dt}{\exp\{y\nu+{\bf B}(y)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}}\bigg|_{y=q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{E^{*}([\tau-I\{Y\leq q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}]{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}^{*}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}}),{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}}\}},

(S.56)

and

\displaystyle\frac{\partial{q^{*}}^{\prime}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}

(S.57)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\tau\int_{0}^{1}t{\bf B}(t)\exp\{t\nu+{\bf B}(t)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}dt}{\exp\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\nu+{\bf B}(y)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\int_{0}^{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})}t{\bf B}(t)\exp\{t\nu+{\bf B}(t)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}dt+q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\exp[q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\nu+{\bf B}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}]\frac{\partial q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}}{\exp[q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\nu+{\bf B}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}]}

\displaystyle-\frac{\tau\int_{0}^{1}t\exp\{t\nu+{\bf B}(t)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}dt-\int_{0}^{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})}t\exp\{t\nu+{\bf B}(t)^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\}dt}{\exp[q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\nu+{\bf B}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}]}

\displaystyle\times\left[\frac{\partial q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}\nu+{\bf B}^{\prime}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}\frac{\partial q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}+{\bf B}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{E^{*}([\tau-I\{Y\leq q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}]Y{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}^{*}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}}),{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}}\}}

\displaystyle-{q^{*}}^{\prime}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}}){\bf B}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}-\frac{\partial q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}[q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})+{q^{*}}^{\prime}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\nu+{q^{*}}^{\prime}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}}){\bf B}^{\prime}\{q^{*}(\nu,{\bm{\gamma}})\}^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}].

Now using (
S.54
), we write
\widehat{{\bm{\eta}}_{\tau}}-{\bm{\eta}}_{\tau 0}
as

\displaystyle\widehat{{\bm{\eta}}_{\tau}}-{\bm{\eta}}_{\tau 0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{q^{*}}^{\prime}\{{\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}\widehat{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\}+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[{q^{*}}^{\prime}\{{\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}\widehat{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\}-{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})\right]

(S.58)

\displaystyle+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})-q^{\prime}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\right\}+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\left[n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}q^{\prime}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})-E\{q^{\prime}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left[n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{q^{*}}^{\prime}\{{\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}\widehat{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\}{\bf I}+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\partial{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}\right](\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})

\displaystyle+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\partial{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}\{\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\}

\displaystyle+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\left[n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}q^{\prime}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})-E\{q^{\prime}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}\right]+O(h^{q}),

where
({{\bm{\beta}}^{*}}^{\rm T},{{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}}^{\rm T})^{\rm T}
is a point on the line
connecting
\{\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})^{\rm T}\}^{\rm T}

.
To treat the first term in (

\displaystyle\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{q^{*}}^{\prime}\{{\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}\widehat{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\}-E\{q^{\prime}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}\right|

(S.59)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|{q^{*}}^{\prime}\{{\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}\widehat{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\}-q^{\prime}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\right|+\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}q^{\prime}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})-E\{q^{\prime}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1)

by (

Now we will show

\displaystyle\Bigg\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\partial{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}-E\Bigg[{\bf X}\Bigg\{\frac{E([\tau-I\{Y\leq q({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}]Y^{2}|{\bf X})}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}\{q({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})|{\bf X}\}}

(S.60)

\displaystyle-2q^{\prime}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})q({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})-\{q^{\prime}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}^{2}[{\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0}+c^{\prime}\{q({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}]\}]\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1).

We prove (

) through proving

\displaystyle\Bigg\|\frac{\partial{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}-{\bf x}\Bigg\{\frac{E([\tau-I\{Y\leq q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}]Y^{2}|{\bf x})}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}\{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})|{\bf x}\}}

(S.61)

\displaystyle-2q^{\prime}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})-\{q^{\prime}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}^{2}[{\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0}+c^{\prime}\{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}]\Bigg\}\Bigg\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1)

uniformly in
{\bf x}
under Condition

,
where
\partial{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})/\partial{\bm{\beta}}
is given in
(

). To prove (

\displaystyle\left|\frac{E^{*}([\tau-I\{Y\leq q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}]Y^{2}|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}^{*}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}),{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}\}}-\frac{E([\tau-I\{Y\leq q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}]Y^{2}|{\bf x})}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}\{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})|{\bf x}\}}\right|

,

\displaystyle|q{{}^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})-q^{\prime}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})|=o_{p}(1)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|{\bf B}^{\prime}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}\|_{2}\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}+|{\bf B}^{\prime}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}-c^{\prime}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}|

\displaystyle+|c^{\prime}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}-c^{\prime}\{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1),

where, to bound the first term, we used

\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}\{(nh)^{-1/2}\}
by Lemma

, for

|{\bf B}^{\prime}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}-c^{\prime}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}|=O(h^{q/2})

under Conditions
(C5)
,
(C8)

and
(C9)
, and for the third term, we noted that

is continuous under Condition

and used (
S.50
).
The results in (

) and (

), together with the

directly lead to

\displaystyle|{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}^{2}[{\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}+{\bf B}^{\prime}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}]-\{q^{\prime}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}^{2}[{\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0}+c^{\prime}\{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}]|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1).

Combining the results in (

), (

), and

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{q^{*}}^{\prime}\{{\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}\widehat{\bm{\beta}},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})\}{\bf I}+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\partial{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}-{\bf C}_{1}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}(1).

(S.66)

Next, we handle the second term in (

).
By (

) and
{\bf u}\in U\equiv\{{\bf u}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}:\|{\bf u}\|_{2}=1\}
, we
have

\displaystyle\left|\left\{\frac{\partial q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}-\frac{E([\tau-I\{Y\leq q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}]{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x})}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}\{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})|{\bf x}\}}\right\}^{\rm T}{\bf u}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{1}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}^{*}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}),{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}\}}\big\{\tau[E^{*}\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}\}-E\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}\}]

\displaystyle-(E^{*}[I\{Y\leq q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}]-E[I\{Y\leq q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}])\big\}\Bigg|

\displaystyle+\Bigg|\left[\frac{1}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}^{*}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}),{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}\}}-\frac{1}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}\{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})|{\bf x}\}}\right]

\displaystyle\times E([\tau-I\{Y\leq q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}]{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x})\Bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(h^{1/2}).

The last equality holds because

and

,

\displaystyle\left|E^{*}[I\{Y\leq q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}]-E[I\{Y\leq q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}{\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}{\bf u}|{\bf x}]\right|=o_{p}(h^{1/2})

,

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}^{*}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}),{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}\}}-\frac{1}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}\{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})|{\bf x}\}}\right|=o_{p}(1)

(S.67)

by Lemma

. Hence we get

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}-\frac{E([\tau-I\{Y\leq q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}]{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x})}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}\{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})|{\bf x}\}}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}(h^{1/2})

uniformly in

Similarly, we have

(S.69)

and

\displaystyle\Bigg\|\frac{E^{*}([\tau-I\{Y\leq q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}]Y{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}^{*}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}),{\bf x},{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}\}}

(S.70)

\displaystyle-\frac{E([\tau-I\{Y\leq q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}]Y{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x})}{f_{Y\mid{\bf X}}\{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})|{\bf x}\}}\Bigg\|_{2}

uniformly in
{\bf x}
. In addition, (

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}){\bf B}\{q^{*}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}-E\left[q^{\prime}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0}){\bf B}\{q({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{{\bf u}\in U}\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}){\bf B}\{q^{*}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}^{\rm T}{\bf u}-E\left[{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}){\bf B}\{q^{*}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}^{\rm T}{\bf u}\right]\right|

\displaystyle+\sup_{{\bf u}\in U}\left|E\left[{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}){\bf B}\{q^{*}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}^{\rm T}{\bf u}\right]-E\left[q^{\prime}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0}){\bf B}\{q({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}^{\rm T}{\bf u}\right]\right|

\displaystyle o_{p}(h^{1/2}).

The last equality in (

\displaystyle\left|E\left[q^{\prime}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0}){\bf B}\{q({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}^{\rm T}{\bf u}\right]\right|

(S.72)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\{\nu:\nu={\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bf x}\in\mathcal{X}\}}{\bf B}\{q(\nu)\}^{\rm T}{\bf u}\left\{\int_{\{{\bf x}:{\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0}=\nu\}}f_{{\bf X}}({\bf x})d{\bf x}\right\}q^{\prime}(\nu)d\nu\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{1}{\bf B}(t)^{\rm T}{\bf u}\left\{\int_{\{{\bf x}:q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})=t\}}f_{{\bf X}}({\bf x})d{\bf x}\right\}dt\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|{\bf B}(\cdot)^{\rm T}{\bf u}\|_{1}\sup_{{\bf x}\in\mathcal{X}}f_{{\bf X}}({\bf x})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(h^{1/2}).

by Lemma
1
,
and similarly

,
which leads to the last step in (
S.71
) under Condition

.
Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle|[q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})+{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}){\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*}+{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}){\bf B}^{\prime}\{q^{*}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})\}^{\rm T}{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}]

(S.73)

\displaystyle-[q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})+q^{\prime}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0}){\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0}+q^{\prime}({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})c^{\prime}\{q({\bf x}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}]|

\displaystyle o_{p}(1)

by (

), (

), (

.
Now, combining

(S.74)

Combining (

\displaystyle\left\|{\bm{\beta}}_{0}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\partial{q^{*}}^{\prime}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}-{\bf C}_{2}\right\|_{2}=o_{p}(h^{1/2}).

(S.75)

Inserting
(
S.66
) and (
S.75
) in (
S.58
), using

, we get

\displaystyle\widehat{{\bm{\eta}}_{\tau}}-{\bm{\eta}}_{\tau 0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf C}_{1}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})+{\bf C}_{2}\{\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}})-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\}

\displaystyle+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\left[n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}q^{\prime}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})-E\{q^{\prime}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}\right]+{\bf r}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf C}{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y|{\bf x}_{i})\}\\
{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf x}_{i}\}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\left[n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}q^{\prime}({\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})-E\{q^{\prime}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}\right]+{\bf C}_{1}{\bf r}_{2}+{\bf C}_{2}{\bf r}_{3}+{\bf r},

where

by Proposition

,
hence

\displaystyle\|{\bf C}_{1}{\bf r}_{2}+{\bf C}_{2}{\bf r}_{3}+{\bf r}\|_{2}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2})

by Lemma
