and (
S.74
). In addition, we obviously have

\displaystyle\hbox{cov}[{\bf X}\{Y-E(Y|{\bf X})\},q^{\prime}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf 0}_{p},

\displaystyle\hbox{cov}[{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)|{\bf X}\},q^{\prime}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf 0}_{m}.

Thus with
{\bf\Sigma}_{{\bm{\eta}}_{\tau}}={\bf C}{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}{\bf C}^{\rm T}+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}{\bm{\beta}}_{0}^{\rm T}\hbox{var}\{q^{\prime}({\bf X}^{\rm T}{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\}
defined in Theorem
2
,

{\bf\Sigma}_{{\bm{\eta}}_{\tau}}^{-1/2}\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\bm{\eta}}_{\tau}-{\bm{\eta}}_{\tau 0})
converges
to the normal distribution with mean
{\bf 0}
and variance
{\bf I}
.
∎

S.9
Proof of Proposition
3

In terms of estimating
{\bm{\beta}}
, the score function is

{\bf S}_{\bm{\beta}}=y{\bf x}-E(Y{\bf x}\mid{\bf x})
and the nuisance tangent space is

\displaystyle\Lambda

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle[{\bf a}(y)-E\{{\bf a}(Y)\mid{\bf x}\}+{\bf b}({\bf x}):\forall{\bf a}(y),{\bf b}({\bf x})\in{\cal R}^{p},E\{{\bf b}({\bf X})\}={\bf 0}].

Its orthogonal complement is

\displaystyle\Lambda^{\perp}=({\bf a}(y,{\bf x})-E\{{\bf a}(Y,{\bf x})\mid{\bf x}\}:E\{{\bf a}(y,{\bf X})\mid y\}=E[E\{{\bf a}(Y,{\bf X})\mid{\bf X}\}\mid y]).

Thus, the efficient score is

{\bf S}_{\rm eff}=y{\bf x}-{\bf a}_{0}(y)-E\{Y{\bf x}-{\bf a}_{0}(Y)\mid{\bf x}\}
, where

{\bf a}_{0}(y)
satisfies

\displaystyle{\bf a}_{0}(y)-E[E\{{\bf a}_{0}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}\mid y]=E(y{\bf X}\mid y)-E\{E(Y{\bf X}\mid{\bf X})\mid y\}.

(S.76)

Thus, the efficient variance is
\{E({\bf S}_{\rm eff}^{\otimes 2})\}^{-1}
.

To show that the MLE estimator
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
is actually efficient, we only
need to show

{\bf\Sigma}^{*}\to E({\bf S}_{\rm eff}^{\otimes 2})
when
n\to\infty
.
Let
{\bf a}_{0}(y)=\Lambda{\bf B}(y)+O(h^{q})
, where
\Lambda
is a
p\times m

coefficient matrix. Then (
S.76
) implies that

\displaystyle\Lambda E\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\otimes 2}\}-\Lambda E[E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}^{\otimes 2}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E[E\{Y{\bf X}-E(Y{\bf X}\mid{\bf X}){\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}\mid{\bf X}\}]+O(h^{q})E\{{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E[{\bf X}\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]+O(h^{q})E\{{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)\},

i.e.

\displaystyle{\bf a}_{0}(y)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Lambda{\bf B}(y)={\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}{\bf B}(y)+O(h^{q})E\{{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)\}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}{\bf B}(y)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}{\bf B}(y)+O(h^{q-1/2}),

where we used
\|E\{{\bf B}(Y)\}\|_{2}=O(h^{1/2}),\|{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}\|_{2}\asymp h^{-1}
,
and
\|{\bf B}(\cdot)\|_{2}=O(1)
.
Therefore

\displaystyle E({\bf S}_{\rm eff}^{\otimes 2})-{\bf\Sigma}^{*}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\left\{(Y{\bf X}-E(Y{\bf X}\mid{\bf X})-{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}[{\bf B}(y)-E\{B(Y)\mid{\bf X}])^{\otimes 2}\right\}-{\bf\Sigma}^{*}+O(h^{q-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(1).

∎

S.10
Proof of Theorem
3

Since

\displaystyle E(\bm{\phi}_{\rm eff}^{\otimes 2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\left([{\bm{\beta}}v({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-{\bm{\beta}}E\{v({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}]^{\otimes 2}\right)

\displaystyle+E\left([{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)+{\bf M}{\bf X}Y-E\{{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)+{\bf M}{\bf X}Y\mid{\bf X}\}]^{\otimes 2}\right),

and

\displaystyle{\bf\Sigma}_{\bm{\xi}}={\bf A}{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}{\bf A}^{\rm T}+{\bm{\beta}}^{\otimes 2}\hbox{var}\{v({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\},

so we only need to show

\displaystyle E\left([{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)+{\bf M}{\bf X}Y-E\{{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)+{\bf M}{\bf X}Y\mid{\bf X}\}]^{\otimes 2}\right)-{\bf A}{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}{\bf A}^{\rm T}\to{\bf 0}.

Now we have
{\bm{\beta}}y^{2}+{\bf a}(y)={\bf\Lambda}{\bf B}(y)+O(h^{q})
, where
{\bf\Lambda}\in{\cal R}^{p\times m}
. Then (
S.5
) and (
S.6
) imply

\displaystyle-{\bf\Lambda}E[{\bf B}(y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}\mid y]+{\bm{\beta}}E\{y^{2}-E(Y^{2}\mid{\bf X})\mid y\}+O(h^{q})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2{\bm{\beta}}E[yE(Y\mid{\bf X})-E\{YE(Y\mid{\bf X})\mid{\bf X}\}\mid y]+{\bf M}E[{\bf X}\{y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\mid y]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2{\bm{\beta}}E[yE(Y\mid{\bf X})-E\{YE(Y\mid{\bf X})\mid{\bf X}\}\mid y]

\displaystyle+\left(E\{v({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf I}-E\left[2{\bm{\beta}}{\bf X}^{\rm T}Yv({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})+\{{\bf\Lambda}{\bf B}(Y)-{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}\}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}{\bf X}^{\rm T}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\times{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}E[{\bf X}\{y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\mid y].

Multiplying
{\bf B}^{\rm T}(y)
on both sides and taking expectation lead
to

\displaystyle-{\bf\Lambda}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}+{\bm{\beta}}E[\{Y^{2}-E(Y^{2}\mid{\bf X})\}{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)]+O(h^{q})E\{{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2{\bm{\beta}}E[YE(Y\mid{\bf X}){\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)-\{E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}^{2}{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)]

\displaystyle+\left(E\{v({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf I}-E\left[2{\bm{\beta}}{\bf X}^{\rm T}Yv({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})+\{{\bf\Lambda}{\bf B}(Y)-{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}\}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf X}^{\rm T}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\times{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}E[{\bf X}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2{\bm{\beta}}E[Y\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]+E\{v({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-E\left\{2{\bm{\beta}}{\bf X}^{\rm T}Yv({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\right\}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}

\displaystyle-{\bf\Lambda}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}+{\bm{\beta}}E[Y^{2}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf X}^{\rm T}]{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2{\bm{\beta}}E[Y\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]+{\bf A}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf\Lambda}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12},

hence

\displaystyle-{\bf\Lambda}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}+{\bf\Lambda}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2{\bm{\beta}}E[Y\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]+{\bf A}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bm{\beta}}E[\{Y^{2}-E(Y^{2}\mid{\bf X})\}{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)]

\displaystyle+O(h^{q})E\{{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf A}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf A}_{2}+O(h^{q})E\{{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)\},

and

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}y^{2}+{\bf a}(y)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf A}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf A}_{2})({\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf\Sigma}_{22})^{-1}{\bf B}(y)

\displaystyle+O(h^{q})E\{{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)\}({\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf\Sigma}_{22})^{-1}{\bf B}(y)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf U}{\bf B}(y)+O(h^{q-1/2}),

where, for notational brevity,

\displaystyle{\bf U}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle({\bf A}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf A}_{2})({\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf\Sigma}_{22})^{-1}.

The above holds by
\|({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}\|_{2}=O(h^{-1})
.
This is because by Conditions
(C6)
and
(C7)
,
all eigenvalues of
{\bf\Sigma}
are of order either 1 or
h
,
which implies
{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}
has all eigenvalues either of order 1 or
h^{-1}
.
Since
({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}
is a block diagonal element of
{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}
,
its eigenvalues are of order 1 or
h^{-1}
as well.
Then

\displaystyle{\bf M}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(E\{v({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf I}-E\{2{\bm{\beta}}{\bf X}^{\rm T}Yv({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}-{\bf U}E\left[{\bf B}(Y)\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf X}^{\rm T}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.+{\bm{\beta}}E\left[Y^{2}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf X}^{\rm T}\right]\right){\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}+O(h^{q-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(E\{v({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf I}-E\{2{\bm{\beta}}{\bf X}^{\rm T}Yv({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}-{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}+{\bm{\beta}}E\left[Y^{2}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}{\bf X}^{\rm T}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\times{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}+O(h^{q-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf A}_{1}-{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}){\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}+O(h^{q-1/2}).

Hence

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}y^{2}+{\bf a}(y)-E\{{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)\mid{\bf x}\}={\bf U}[{\bf B}(y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}\}]+O(h^{q-1/2}),

and

\displaystyle E\{\hbox{var}({\bf M}{\bf X}Y\mid{\bf X})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf M}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}{\bf M}^{\rm T},

\displaystyle E[\hbox{var}\{{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}{\bf U}^{\rm T}+O(h^{q-1/2}),

\displaystyle E[\hbox{cov}\{{\bf M}{\bf X}Y,{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf M}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf U}^{\rm T}+O(h^{q-1/2}).

Thus, noting that
{\bf A}=[{\bf A}_{1},{\bf A}_{2}]
,

\displaystyle E\left([{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)+{\bf M}{\bf X}Y-E\{{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)+{\bf M}{\bf X}Y\mid{\bf X}\}]^{\otimes 2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf M}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}{\bf M}^{\rm T}+{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}{\bf U}^{\rm T}+{\bf M}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf U}^{\rm T}+{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf M}^{\rm T}+O(h^{q-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf A}_{1}-{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}){\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}({\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}-{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf U}^{\rm T})+{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}{\bf U}^{\rm T}+({\bf A}_{1}-{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}){\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf U}^{\rm T}

\displaystyle+{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}({\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}-{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf U}^{\rm T})+O(h^{q-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf A}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}+{\bf U}({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}){\bf U}^{\rm T}+O(h^{q-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf A}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}+({\bf A}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf A}_{2})({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}({\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}-{\bf A}_{2}^{\rm T})

\displaystyle+O(h^{q-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf A}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}+{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}&-{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}\\
-({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}&({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}\end{array}\right){\bf A}^{\rm T}

\displaystyle+O(h^{q-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf A}{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}{\bf A}^{\rm T}+o(1).

∎

S.11
Proof of Theorem
4

By (
S.12
)

\displaystyle E(\bm{\phi}_{\rm eff}^{\otimes 2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\left([{\bm{\beta}}q^{\prime}(\nu)-{\bm{\beta}}E\{q^{\prime}(\nu)\}]^{\otimes 2}\right)

\displaystyle+E\left([{\bf M}_{1}{\bf X}Y+{\bf a}(Y)-E\{{\bf M}_{1}{\bf X}Y+{\bf a}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]^{\otimes 2}\right),

and

\displaystyle{\bf\Sigma}_{{\bm{\eta}}_{\tau}}={\bf C}{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}{\bf C}^{\rm T}+{\bm{\beta}}^{\otimes 2}\hbox{var}\{q^{\prime}(\nu)\}

in Theorem
2
,
we only need to show

\displaystyle E\left([{\bf M}_{1}{\bf X}Y+{\bf a}(Y)-E\{{\bf M}_{1}{\bf X}Y+{\bf a}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]^{\otimes 2}\right)-{\bf C}{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}{\bf C}^{\rm T}\to{\bf 0}.

Now we have
{\bf a}(y)={\bf\Lambda}{\bf B}(y)+O(h^{q})
where
{\bf\Lambda}\in{\cal R}^{p\times m}
.
Then (
S.11
) and (
S.13
) imply

\displaystyle-{\bf\Lambda}E[{\bf B}(y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}\mid y]+O(h^{q})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\bm{\beta}}E\{r(y,\nu)\mid y\}+{\bf M}_{1}E[{\bf X}\{y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\mid y]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\bm{\beta}}E\{r(y,\nu)\mid y\}

\displaystyle+\left(E\{q^{\prime}(\nu)\}{\bf I}+{\bm{\beta}}E\{{\bf X}^{\rm T}q^{\prime\prime}(\nu)\}-E\left[\{{\bf\Lambda}{\bf B}(Y)+O(h^{q})\}{\bf X}^{\rm T}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\times{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}E[{\bf X}\{y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\mid y].

Multiplying
{\bf B}^{\rm T}(y)
on both sides above and taking expectation,
incorporating (
S.7
),
we get

\displaystyle-{\bf\Lambda}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}+O(h^{q})E\{{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\bm{\beta}}E\{r(Y,\nu){\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)\}

\displaystyle+\left(E\{q^{\prime}(\nu)\}{\bf I}+{\bm{\beta}}E\{{\bf X}^{\rm T}q^{\prime\prime}(\nu)\}-E\left[\{{\bf\Lambda}{\bf B}(Y)+O(h^{q})\}{\bf X}^{\rm T}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\times{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}E[{\bf X}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\bm{\beta}}E\Bigg(\frac{E\{\epsilon Y{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)|{\bf X}\}}{f\{q(\nu),\nu\}}-q^{\prime}(\nu){\bf B}^{\rm T}\{q(\nu)\}-\frac{E\{\epsilon{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)|{\bf X}\}}{f\{q(\nu),\nu\}}[q(\nu)+q^{\prime}(\nu)\nu+q^{\prime}(\nu)c^{\prime}\{q(\nu)\}]\Bigg)

\displaystyle+{\bf C}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf\Lambda}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}+O(h^{q}){\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-{\bf\Lambda}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}+{\bf C}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf C}_{2}+O(h^{q}){\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12},

hence

\displaystyle{\bf\Lambda}({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})=-{\bf C}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}+{\bf C}_{2}+O(h^{q+1/2}),

since
\|E\{{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)\}\|_{2}=O(h^{1/2})
and
\|{\bf\Sigma}_{12}\|_{2}=O(h^{1/2})
by Lemma
2
.
Then

\displaystyle{\bf a}(y)={\bf U}{\bf B}(y)+O(h^{q-1/2}),

where for notational brevity,

\displaystyle{\bf U}\equiv(-{\bf C}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}+{\bf C}_{2})({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}.

The above holds by
\|({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}\|_{2}=O(h^{-1})

under Conditions
(C6)
and
(C7)
.
Then by (
S.13
),

\displaystyle{\bf M}_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(E\{q^{\prime}(\nu)\}{\bf I}+{\bm{\beta}}E\{{\bf X}^{\rm T}q^{\prime\prime}(\nu)\}-{\bf U}E\left[{\bf B}(Y){\bf X}^{\rm T}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\right]){\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}+O(h^{q-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf C}_{1}-{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}){\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}+O(h^{q-1/2}).

Hence

\displaystyle{\bf a}(y)-E\{{\bf a}(Y)\mid{\bf x}\}={\bf U}[{\bf B}(y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}\}]+O(h^{q-1/2}),

and

\displaystyle E\{\hbox{var}({\bf M}_{1}{\bf X}Y\mid{\bf X})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf M}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}{\bf M}_{1}^{\rm T},

\displaystyle E[\hbox{cov}\{{\bf M}{\bf X}Y,{\bf a}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf M}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf U}^{\rm T}+O(h^{q-1/2}),

\displaystyle E[\hbox{var}\{{\bf a}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}{\bf U}^{\rm T}+O(h^{q-1/2}).

Thus noting that
{\bf C}=[{\bf C}_{1},{\bf C}_{2}]
, we obtain

\displaystyle E\left([{\bf M}_{1}{\bf X}Y+{\bf a}(Y)-E\{{\bf M}_{1}{\bf X}Y+{\bf a}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]^{\otimes 2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf M}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}{\bf M}_{1}^{\rm T}+{\bf M}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf U}^{\rm T}+{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf M}_{1}^{\rm T}+{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}{\bf U}^{\rm T}+O(h^{q-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf C}_{1}-{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}){\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}({\bf C}_{1}^{\rm T}-{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf U}^{\rm T})+({\bf C}_{1}-{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}){\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf U}^{\rm T}

\displaystyle+{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}({\bf C}_{1}^{\rm T}-{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf U}^{\rm T})+{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}{\bf U}^{\rm T}+O(h^{q-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf C}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf C}_{1}^{\rm T}+{\bf U}({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}){\bf U}^{\rm T}+O(h^{q-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf C}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf C}_{1}^{\rm T}+(-{\bf C}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}+{\bf C}_{2})({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}(-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf C}_{1}^{\rm T}+{\bf C}_{2}^{\rm T})

\displaystyle+O(h^{q-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf C}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}+{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}&-{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}\\
-({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}&({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}\end{array}\right){\bf C}^{\rm T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf C}{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}{\bf C}^{\rm T}+o(1).

∎

S.12
Lemmas

We now introduce a lemma for the analysis of the discrete response case.

Lemma 8
.

Let
{\bm{\theta}}^{*}\equiv({\bm{\beta}}^{*\rm T},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*\rm T})^{\rm T}
and

\|{\bm{\theta}}^{*}-{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\|_{2}=o(1)
.
Under Conditions
(D1)
-
(D2)
,
uniformly with respect to
{\bf x}
,

(i)

\|{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\|_{2}=O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}+\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2})
,

(ii)

\|{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\|_{2}=O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}+\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2})
,

(iii)

\|{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\|_{2}=O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}+\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2})
,

(iv)

\|\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*}\}-\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\}\|_{2}=O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}+\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2})
.

If
{\bm{\theta}}^{*}
satisfies
\|{\bm{\theta}}^{*}-{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\|_{2}=o_{p}(1)
,
the above results hold in probability.

Proof.

For some
\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}
on the line connecting
{\bm{\theta}}^{*}
and
{\bm{\theta}}_{0}
,
we have

\displaystyle{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\partial{\bf p}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})+\frac{\partial{\bf p}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}({\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}).

First, by Conditions
(D1)
and
(D2)
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial{\bf p}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left\{{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})-E(Y\mid{\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}){\bf p}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right\}{\bf x}^{\rm T}({\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[\sum_{y=1}^{m}\left\{y-E(Y\mid{\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right\}^{2}\hbox{pr}(Y=y\mid{\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})^{2}\right]^{1/2}O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{y=1}^{m}\left|y-E(Y\mid{\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right|\hbox{pr}(Y=y\mid{\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2E(Y\mid{\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\{E(Y\mid{\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})+O(\|\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}-{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\|_{2})\}O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}).

The last inequality holds since
E(Y\mid{\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})
is bounded by Condition
(D1)
,
and
\|\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}-{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\|_{2}=o(1)
by the definition of
\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}
.
In addition, for a vector
{\bf a}
, let
\hbox{diag}({\bf a})
be the diagonal
matrix with entries equal to the elements of
{\bf a}
, then

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial{\bf p}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}({\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left[\hbox{diag}\left\{{\bf p}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right\}-{\bf p}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}){\bf p}^{\rm T}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right]({\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[\|{\bf p}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\|_{\infty}+\|{\bf p}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\|_{2}^{2}\right]\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2})

because
\|{\bf p}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\|_{\infty}\leq\|{\bf p}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\|_{2}\leq 1
.
Hence, we get

\displaystyle\|{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}+\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}).

Similarly for some
\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}
on the line connecting
{\bm{\theta}}^{*}
and
{\bm{\theta}}_{0}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left\{{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})-E(Y\mid{\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}){\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right\}{\bf x}^{\rm T}({\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{y=1}^{m}\left|y-E(Y\mid{\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right|y\hbox{pr}(Y=y\mid{\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[E(Y^{2}\mid{\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})+\{E(Y\mid{\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\}^{2}+O(\|\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}-{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\|_{2})\right]O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2})

by Conditions
(D1)
and
(D2)
.
In addition,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}({\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left[\hbox{diag}\left\{{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right\}-{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}){\bf p}^{\rm T}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right]({\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[\left\|\hbox{diag}\left\{{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right\}\right\|_{2}+\left\|{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}){\bf p}^{\rm T}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right\|_{2}\right]\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[\|{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},\widetilde{\theta})\|_{\infty}+\|{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\|_{2}\|{\bf p}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\|_{2}\right]\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}),

because
\|{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},\widetilde{\theta})\|_{\infty}\leq\|{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},\widetilde{\theta})\|_{2}\leq\|{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},\widetilde{\theta})\|_{1}=E(Y\mid{\bf x},\widetilde{\theta})=E(Y\mid{\bf x})+o(1)

and
E(Y\mid{\bf x})
is bounded by Condition
(D1)
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\|{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})+\frac{\partial{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}({\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}+\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}).

Also, by Conditions
(D1)
and
(D2)
,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left\{{\bf p}_{3}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})-E(Y\mid{\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}){\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right\}{\bf x}^{\rm T}({\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{y=1}^{m}\left|y-E(Y\mid{\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right|y^{2}\hbox{pr}(Y=y\mid{\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[E(Y^{3}\mid{\bf x})+E(Y\mid{\bf x})E(Y^{2}\mid{\bf x})+O(\|\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}-{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\|_{2})\right]O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}),

and

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}({\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\left[\hbox{diag}\left\{{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right\}-{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}){\bf p}^{\rm T}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right]({\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[\left\|\hbox{diag}\left\{{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right\}\right\|_{2}+\left\|{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}}){\bf p}^{\rm T}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\right\|_{2}\right]\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left[\|{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},\widetilde{\theta})\|_{\infty}+\|{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\|_{2}\|{\bf p}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})\|_{2}\right]\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}).

The last inequality holds because

\|{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},\widetilde{\theta})\|_{\infty}\leq\|{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},\widetilde{\theta})\|_{2}\leq\|{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},\widetilde{\theta})\|_{1}=E(Y^{2}\mid{\bf x},\widetilde{\theta})=E(Y^{2}\mid{\bf x})+o(1)

and
E(Y^{2}\mid{\bf x})
is bounded by Condition
(D1)
.
Then we get

\displaystyle\|{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}({\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})+\frac{\partial{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x},\widetilde{\bm{\theta}})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}({\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}+\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}).

Now,

\displaystyle\left\|\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*}\}-\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\hbox{diag}\left\{{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\right\}\right\|_{2}+\left\|{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*}){\bf p}^{\rm T}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0}){\bf p}^{\rm T}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\left\|{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})\{{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\}^{\rm T}+\{{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\}{\bf p}^{\rm T}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left|\{{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\}^{\rm T}{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})\right|+\left|{\bf p}^{\rm T}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\{{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\left\|{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}({\bf x},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}+\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}).

Since the support of
{\bf x}
is compact by Condition
(D1)
,
the about results hold uniformly with respect to
{\bf x}
.
∎

S.13
Proof of Proposition
4

First, we will show that

\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2})
and
\|\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})
.
Note that the Hessian of
l({\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})
, i.e.

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})/\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}&\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})/\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}\\
\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}&\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})/\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}{\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}})&{\bf x}_{i}\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}\}\\
[{\bf x}_{i}\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}\}]^{\rm T}&\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}\}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{cov}\left\{\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}Y\\
{\bf B}(Y)\end{bmatrix},\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}Y\\
{\bf B}(Y)\end{bmatrix}\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}\right\}

is negative definite for any
{\bm{\theta}}
,
which implies that a local maximizer is the global maximizer.
Hence similarly to the proof of Proposition
1
,
it suffices to show for any
\epsilon>0

there exists constants
C_{\bm{\beta}},C_{\bm{\gamma}}>0
such that

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}\left\{l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})>\sup_{\|{\bf v}_{\bm{\beta}}\|_{2}=C_{\bm{\beta}},\|{\bf v}_{\bm{\gamma}}\|_{2}=C_{\bm{\gamma}}}l({\bm{\beta}}_{0}+n^{-1/2}{\bf v}_{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}+n^{-1/2}m^{1/2}{\bf v}_{\bm{\gamma}})\right\}\geq 1-5\epsilon

(S.79)

for a sufficiently large
n
.
Now, by the Taylor expansion,

\displaystyle l({\bm{\beta}}_{0}+n^{-1/2}{\bf v}_{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}+n^{-1/2}m^{1/2}{\bf v}_{\bm{\gamma}})-l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})

(S.80)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1/2}\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}{\bf v}_{\bm{\beta}}+n^{-1/2}m^{1/2}\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}{\bf v}_{\bm{\gamma}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}n^{-1}{\bf v}_{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}{\bf v}_{\bm{\beta}}+\frac{1}{2}n^{-1}m{\bf v}_{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}{\bf v}_{\bm{\gamma}}+n^{-1}m^{1/2}{\bf v}_{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}{\bf v}_{\bm{\gamma}}

where
{\bm{\beta}}^{*}={\bm{\beta}}_{0}+\alpha_{1}n^{-1/2}{\bf v}_{\bm{\beta}}
and
{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}={\bm{\gamma}}_{0}+\alpha_{2}n^{-1/2}m^{1/2}{\bf v}_{\bm{\gamma}}

for some
{\bf v}_{\bm{\beta}},{\bf v}_{\bm{\gamma}}
such that
\|{\bf v}_{\bm{\beta}}\|_{2}=C_{\bm{\beta}},\|{\bf v}_{\bm{\gamma}}\|_{2}=C_{\bm{\gamma}}

and some
\alpha_{1}\in(0,1),\alpha_{2}\in(0,1)
.
We first have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}\right\|_{2}=\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})\}\right\|_{2}\asymp_{p}n^{1/2}

by Conditions
(D1)
and
(D5)
.
Therefore, for any
\epsilon>0
there exists a constant
0<C_{1}<\infty
such that

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}\left\{\left\|\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}\right\|_{2}\leq C_{1}n^{1/2}\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon.

(S.81)

Also,

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}\right\|_{2}=\left\|\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i}\}]\right\|_{2}\asymp_{p}n^{1/2}m^{-1/2},

because

\displaystyle\left\|E\left\{\left(\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[{\bf B}(Y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}_{i}\}\right]\right)^{\otimes 2}\right\}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|nE\left(\left[{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}\right]^{\otimes 2}\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n\left\|E[\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\asymp

\displaystyle nm^{-1}

by Remark
1
.
Hence, for any
\epsilon>0
there exists a constant
0<C_{2}<\infty
such that

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}\left\{\left\|\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}\right\|_{2}\leq C_{2}n^{1/2}m^{-1/2}\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon.

(S.82)

In addition, noting that

\|{\bm{\theta}}^{*}-{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\|_{2}=O(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})=o(1)
by Condition

(D3)
, we get

\displaystyle\left\|-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}-{\bf\Sigma}_{11}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}{\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}\{\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})\}\right\|_{2}

(S.83)

\displaystyle+\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}{\bf x}_{i}^{\rm T}\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})-E\{{\bf X}{\bf X}^{\rm T}\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(1)

by Conditions
(D1)
and
(D2)
.
Since all eigenvalues of
{\bf\Sigma}_{11}
are of constant order
by Conditions
(D1)
and
(D5)
,
the above implies there exists a constant
0<C_{3}<\infty
such that

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}\left\{{\bf v}_{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}{\bf v}_{\bm{\beta}}\leq-C_{3}C_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}n\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon.

(S.84)

Further, since
\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}=O(n^{-1/2})
and
\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})
,

\displaystyle\left\|-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}-{\bf\Sigma}_{22}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}[\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*}\}-\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i}\}]\right\|_{2}

(S.85)

\displaystyle+\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i}\}-E[\hbox{var}\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{-1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(m^{-1})

by Lemma
8
, Remark
1
,
and
n^{-1/2}m^{1/2}=o(m^{-1})
by Condition
(D3)
.
Together with Remark
1
, this implies

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}\left\{{\bf v}_{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}{\bf v}_{\bm{\gamma}}\leq-C_{4}C_{\bm{\gamma}}^{2}nm^{-1}\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon

(S.86)

for some constant
0<C_{4}<\infty
.
Now, we have
\|{\bf\Sigma}_{12}\|_{2}=O(m^{-1/2})
,
because noting that
{\bf\Sigma}_{11}
and
{\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}

are positive definite by Condition
(D5)
,
for any
{\bf u}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
such that
\|{\bf u}\|_{2}=1
we have

\displaystyle c\|{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf u}\|_{2}^{2}\leq\|{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1/2}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf u}\|_{2}^{2}={\bf u}^{\rm T}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf u}<{\bf u}^{\rm T}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}{\bf u}\leq\|{\bf\Sigma}_{22}\|_{2}\asymp m^{-1}

(S.87)

by Remark
1
for some constant
c>0
.
Also, since
\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}=O(n^{-1/2})
and
\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})
,

\displaystyle\left\|-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}-{\bf\Sigma}_{12}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}[\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*}\}-\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i}\}]\right\|_{2}

(S.88)

\displaystyle+\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i}\}-E[{\bf X}\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\{{\bf p}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-{\bf p}_{1}^{\rm T}({\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\{E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*}){\bf p}^{\rm T}({\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}_{0}){\bf p}^{\rm T}({\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})\}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i}\}-E[{\bf X}\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(m^{-1/2})

by Lemma
8
, Condition
(D1)
,
and
n^{-1/2}m^{1/2}=o(m^{-1/2})
by Condition
(D3)
.
Thus we have

\displaystyle\hbox{pr}\left\{{\bf v}_{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}{\bf v}_{\bm{\gamma}}\leq C_{5}C_{\bm{\beta}}C_{\bm{\gamma}}nm^{-1/2}\right\}\geq 1-\epsilon

(S.89)

for some constant
0<C_{5}<\infty
.
Combining (
S.80
), (
S.81
), (
S.82
),
(
S.84
), (
S.86
), and (
S.89
),
with probability at least
1-5\epsilon
,

\displaystyle l({\bm{\beta}}_{0}+n^{-1/2}{\bf v}_{\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}+n^{-1/2}m^{1/2}{\bf v}_{\bm{\gamma}})-l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{1}C_{\bm{\beta}}+C_{2}C_{\bm{\gamma}}-\frac{C_{3}}{2}C_{\bm{\beta}}^{2}-\frac{C_{4}}{2}C_{\bm{\gamma}}^{2}+C_{5}C_{\bm{\beta}}C_{\bm{\gamma}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle C_{\bm{\beta}}\left(C_{1}-\frac{C_{3}}{2}C_{\bm{\beta}}+C_{5}C_{\bm{\gamma}}\right)+C_{\bm{\gamma}}\left(C_{2}-\frac{C_{4}}{2}C_{\bm{\gamma}}\right)

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle 0

when
C_{\bm{\beta}}>2(C_{1}+C_{5}C_{\bm{\gamma}})/C_{3}
and
C_{\bm{\gamma}}>2C_{2}/C_{4}
, and this proves (
S.79
).
Therefore,
\|\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2})
and
\|\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})
.

Now, we analyze the asymptotic behavior of
(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T})^{\rm T}
.
Since
(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T})^{\rm T}
is the maximizer of
l({\bm{\beta}},{\bm{\gamma}})
,
letting
({\bm{\beta}}^{*\rm T},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*\rm T})^{\rm T}
be on the line connecting

(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T},\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T})^{\rm T}
and
({\bm{\beta}}_{0}^{\rm T},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}^{\rm T})^{\rm T}
,

\displaystyle{\bf 0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\frac{\partial l({\bm{\beta}}_{0},{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})}{\partial{\bm{\theta}}}-\left\{-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\theta}}\partial{\bm{\theta}}^{\rm T}}\right\}\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\\
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})\}\\
{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i}\}\end{bmatrix}-\left({\bf\Sigma}+\begin{bmatrix}{\bf R}_{11}&{\bf R}_{12}\\
{\bf R}_{12}^{\rm T}&{\bf R}_{22}\end{bmatrix}\right)\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\\
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\end{bmatrix},

where

\displaystyle{\bf R}_{11}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}-{\bf\Sigma}_{11},

\displaystyle{\bf R}_{12}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}-{\bf\Sigma}_{12},

\displaystyle{\bf R}_{22}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle-n^{-1}\frac{\partial^{2}l({\bm{\beta}}^{*},{\bm{\gamma}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}-{\bf\Sigma}_{22}.

Since
{\bf\Sigma}
is invertible by Condition
(D5)
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\\
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\end{bmatrix}={\bf\Sigma}^{-1}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})\}\\
{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i}\}\end{bmatrix}-{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}{\bf R}_{11}&{\bf R}_{12}\\
{\bf R}_{12}^{\rm T}&{\bf R}_{22}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\\
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\end{bmatrix}.

Now, note that

\displaystyle{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}=\begin{bmatrix}{\bf\Sigma}_{\bm{\beta}}&-{\bf\Sigma}_{\bm{\beta}}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}\\
-{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{\bm{\beta}}&{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}+{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{\bm{\beta}}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}O(1)&O(m^{1/2})\\
O(m^{1/2})&O(m)\end{bmatrix}

(S.90)

in terms of the 2-norms of the block matrices.
This is because
{\bf\Sigma}_{\bm{\beta}}^{-1}={\bf\Sigma}_{11}-{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}

is positive definite by Condition
(D5)
,

\|{\bf\Sigma}_{12}\|_{2}=O(m^{-1/2})
by (
S.87
),
and
\|{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}\|_{2}\asymp m
by Remark
1
.
In addition, using the fact that

\|{\bm{\beta}}^{*}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2})
and
\|{\bm{\gamma}}^{*}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})
,
similar arguments to (
S.83
), (
S.85
), and
(
S.88
) leads to

\|{\bf R}_{11}\|_{2}=o_{p}(1)
,

\|{\bf R}_{22}\|_{2}=o_{p}(m^{-1})
and
\|{\bf R}_{12}\|_{2}=o_{p}(m^{-1/2})
.
Hence, in terms of their 2-norms,

\displaystyle-{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}\begin{bmatrix}{\bf R}_{11}&{\bf R}_{12}\\
{\bf R}_{12}^{\rm T}&{\bf R}_{22}\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\\
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}O(1)&O(m^{1/2})\\
O(m^{1/2})&O(m)\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}o_{p}(1)&o_{p}(m^{-1/2})\\
o_{p}(m^{-1/2})&o_{p}(m^{-1})\end{bmatrix}\begin{bmatrix}O_{p}(n^{-1/2})\\
O_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})\end{bmatrix}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}o_{p}(n^{-1/2})\\
o_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})\end{bmatrix}.

Therefore we get

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\\
\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0}\end{bmatrix}={\bf\Sigma}^{-1}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})\}\\
{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i}\}\end{bmatrix}+\begin{bmatrix}{\bf r}_{1}\\
{\bf r}_{2}\end{bmatrix},

where
\|{\bf r}_{1}\|_{2}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2})
and
\|{\bf r}_{2}\|_{2}=o_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})
,
which proves the second result of Proposition
4
.
Also, we can express
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}
as

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}{\bf I}_{p}&{\bf 0}_{m}\end{bmatrix}{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})\}\\
{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i}\}\end{bmatrix}+{\bf r}_{1}.

Then since

\displaystyle E\left(\begin{bmatrix}{\bf X}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\\
{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)={\bf\Sigma},

using (
S.90
) we can conclude

{\bf\Sigma}_{\bm{\beta}}^{-1/2}\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})\to N({\bf 0}_{p},{\bf I}_{p})
in distribution as
n\to\infty
.
∎

S.14
Proof of Theorem
5

We can express
\widehat{\bm{\xi}}-{\bm{\xi}}_{0}
as

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\xi}}-{\bm{\xi}}_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},\widehat{\bm{\theta}})+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\{\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},\widehat{\bm{\theta}})-\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})\}

(S.91)

\displaystyle+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\left[n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})-E\{\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},\widehat{\bm{\theta}}){\bf I}+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\partial\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}}\right\}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})

\displaystyle+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\partial\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}^{\rm T}}(\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})

\displaystyle+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\left[n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})-E\{\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\right],

where
{\bm{\theta}}^{*}
is on the line connecting
\widehat{\bm{\theta}}
and
{\bm{\theta}}_{0}
.
Since
\|\widehat{\bm{\theta}}-{\bm{\theta}}_{0}\|_{2}=O_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})
by Proposition
4

and
\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}})
is Lipschitz continuous at
{\bm{\theta}}_{0}
uniformly with respect to
{\bf x}

by Conditions
(D1)
and
(D2)
,
it is easy to see that

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},\widehat{\bm{\theta}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})

(S.92)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\{\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf X})\}+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2}).

Similarly, Conditions
(D1)
and
(D2)
lead to

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\partial\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\beta}}}-E\left[{\bf X}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}^{3}\right]\right\|_{2}

(S.93)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}{\bf x}_{i}E[\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})\}^{3}\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*}]-E\left[{\bf X}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}^{3}\right]\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2}).

In addition, in terms of the 2-norm, we have

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\partial\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[E\{{\bf B}(Y)Y^{2}\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*}\}-2E\{{\bf B}(Y)Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*}\}E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*}\}E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})^{2}-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*}\}E(Y^{2}\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})-2{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})+2{\bf p}({\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})^{2}-{\bf p}({\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})E(Y^{2}\mid{\bf x}_{i})\right\}

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{{\bf p}_{2}({\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})-2{\bf p}_{1}({\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})+2{\bf p}({\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})^{2}-{\bf p}({\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}_{0})E(Y^{2}\mid{\bf x}_{i})\right\}

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}E([{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i}\}]\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})\}^{2}\mid{\bf x}_{i})+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E([{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}^{2})

\displaystyle+n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}E([{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i}\}]\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})\}^{2}\mid{\bf x}_{i})-E([{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}^{2})

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2}).

The second equality holds by Conditions
(D1)
and
(D2)
,
and the third equality holds by Lemma
8
.
Now let
{\bf W}_{1}\equiv{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}
and
W_{2}\equiv\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}^{2}
,
then we can show
\|E({\bf W}_{1}W_{2})\|_{2}=O(m^{-1/2})
since for any
{\bf u}\in\mathbb{R}^{m}
such that
\|{\bf u}\|_{2}=1
,

\displaystyle\{E({\bf W}_{1}W_{2})^{\rm T}{\bf u}\}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{\rm T}E\{\hbox{cov}({\bf W}_{1},W_{2}\mid{\bf X})\}E\{\hbox{cov}(W_{2},{\bf W}_{1}\mid{\bf X})\}{\bf u}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{\rm T}E\{\hbox{cov}({\bf W}_{1},W_{2}\mid{\bf X})\hbox{cov}(W_{2},{\bf W}_{1}\mid{\bf X})\}{\bf u}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\bf u}^{\rm T}E\{\hbox{var}({\bf W}_{1}\mid{\bf X})\hbox{var}(W_{2}\mid{\bf X})\}{\bf u}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C{\bf u}^{\rm T}E\{\hbox{var}({\bf W}_{1}\mid{\bf X})\}{\bf u}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(m^{-1})

for some constant
C>0
.
The fourth argument holds since
\hbox{var}(W_{2}\mid{\bf x})
is uniformly bounded
by Condition
(D1)
,
and the last argument holds because

\|E\{\hbox{var}({\bf W}_{1}\mid{\bf X})\}\|_{2}=\|{\bf\Sigma}_{22}\|_{2}\asymp m^{-1}
by Remark

1
.
This leads to

\displaystyle\|{\bf A}_{2}\|_{2}=\|{\bm{\beta}}_{0}E({\bf W}_{1}W_{2})^{\rm T}\|_{2}=\|{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\|_{2}\|E({\bf W}_{1}W_{2})\|_{2}=O(m^{-1/2}).

(S.94)

Similarly,

\displaystyle E\left[\left\{m^{1/2}E({\bf W}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf u}W_{2}\mid{\bf X})\right\}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle mE\left[\left\{\hbox{cov}({\bf W}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf u},W_{2}\mid{\bf X})\right\}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle mE\left\{\hbox{var}({\bf W}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf u}\mid{\bf X})\hbox{var}(W_{2}\mid{\bf X})\right\}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle Cm{\bf u}^{\rm T}E\left\{\hbox{var}({\bf W}_{1}\mid{\bf X})\right\}{\bf u}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(1),

i.e. the second moment of
m^{1/2}E({\bf W}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf u}W_{2}\mid{\bf X})
is finite, then

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}E({\bf W}_{1}W_{2}\mid{\bf x}_{i})-E({\bf W}_{1}W_{2})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle m^{-1/2}\sup_{{\bf u}\in{\bf R}^{m}:\|{\bf u}\|_{2}=1}\left|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}m^{1/2}E({\bf W}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf u}W_{2}\mid{\bf x}_{i})-m^{1/2}E({\bf W}_{1}^{\rm T}{\bf u}W_{2})\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O_{p}(m^{-1/2}n^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(m^{-1/2}).

Thus using
n^{-1/2}m^{1/2}=o(m^{-1/2})
under Condition
(D3)
, we get

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\partial\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i},{\bm{\theta}}^{*})}{\partial{\bm{\gamma}}}-E([{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}^{2})\right\|_{2}

(S.95)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}E({\bf W}_{1}W_{2}\mid{\bf x}_{i})-E({\bf W}_{1}W_{2})\right\|_{2}+O_{p}(n^{-1/2}m^{1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o_{p}(m^{-1/2}).

Combining the results of Proposition
4
,
(
S.91
), (
S.92
), (
S.93
), (
S.94
),
and (
S.95
),
in terms of the 2-norm we get

\displaystyle\widehat{\bm{\xi}}-{\bm{\xi}}_{0}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf A}_{1}(\widehat{\bm{\beta}}-{\bm{\beta}}_{0})+{\bf A}_{2}(\widehat{\bm{\gamma}}-{\bm{\gamma}}_{0})+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\left[n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})-E\{\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\right]

\displaystyle+O_{p}(n^{-1}m^{1/2})+o_{p}(n^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf A}{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})\}\\
{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i}\}\end{bmatrix}+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\left[n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})-E\{\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\right]

\displaystyle+{\bf A}_{1}{\bf r}_{1}+{\bf A}_{2}{\bf r}_{2}+o_{p}(n^{-1/2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf A}{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\begin{bmatrix}{\bf x}_{i}\{y_{i}-E(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})\}\\
{\bf B}(y_{i})-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}_{i}\}\end{bmatrix}+{\bm{\beta}}_{0}\left[n^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf x}_{i})-E\{\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\right]

\displaystyle+o_{p}(n^{-1/2})

since
m=o(n^{1/3})
by Condition
(D3)
.
Since

\displaystyle E\left(\begin{bmatrix}{\bf X}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\\
{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}\end{bmatrix}^{\otimes 2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf\Sigma},

\displaystyle\hbox{cov}[{\bf X}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\},\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf X})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf 0},

\displaystyle\hbox{cov}[{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\},\hbox{var}(Y\mid{\bf X})]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf 0},

we can conclude

{\bf\Sigma}_{\bm{\xi}}^{-1/2}\sqrt{n}(\widehat{\bm{\xi}}-{\bm{\xi}}_{0})\to N({\bf 0},{\bf I})
in distribution as
n\to\infty
.
∎

S.15
Proof of Proposition
5

The efficient score for
{\bm{\beta}}
given in Appendix
S.9
is

{\bf S}_{\rm eff}=y{\bf x}-{\bf a}_{0}(y)-E\{Y{\bf x}-{\bf a}_{0}(Y)\mid{\bf x}\}
, where

{\bf a}_{0}(y)
satisfies

\displaystyle{\bf a}_{0}(y)-E[E\{{\bf a}_{0}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}\mid y]=E(y{\bf X}\mid y)-E\{E(Y{\bf X}\mid{\bf X})\mid y\}.

(S.96)

Thus, the efficient variance is
\{E({\bf S}_{\rm eff}^{\otimes 2})\}^{-1}
.

To show that the MLE estimator
\widehat{\bm{\beta}}
in Proposition
4
is efficient, we
need to show

{\bf\Sigma}_{\bm{\beta}}^{-1}=E({\bf S}_{\rm eff}^{\otimes 2})
.

{\bf a}_{0}(y)
must be of the general form
\Lambda{\bf B}(y)
,
i.e.
{\bf a}_{0}(y)=\Lambda{\bf B}(y)
,
where
\Lambda
is a
p\times m

coefficient matrix. Then (
S.96
) implies that

\displaystyle\Lambda E\{{\bf B}(Y)^{\otimes 2}\}-\Lambda E[E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}^{\otimes 2}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E[E\{Y{\bf X}-E(Y{\bf X}\mid{\bf X}){\bf B}(Y)^{\rm T}\mid{\bf X}\}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E[{\bf X}\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}],

i.e.,

\displaystyle{\bf a}_{0}(y)=\Lambda{\bf B}(y)={\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}{\bf B}(y).

Therefore,

\displaystyle E({\bf S}_{\rm eff}^{\otimes 2})=E\left\{(Y{\bf X}-E(Y{\bf X}\mid{\bf X})-{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}^{-1}[{\bf B}(Y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}])^{\otimes 2}\right\}={\bf\Sigma}_{\bm{\beta}}^{-1}.

∎

S.16
Proof of Theorem
6

Since the efficient score for
{\bm{\xi}}
given in Section
2.1
leads to

\displaystyle E(\bm{\phi}_{\rm eff}^{\otimes 2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle E\left([{\bm{\beta}}v({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})-{\bm{\beta}}E\{v({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}]^{\otimes 2}\right)

\displaystyle+E\left([{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)+{\bf M}{\bf X}Y-E\{{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)+{\bf M}{\bf X}Y\mid{\bf X}\}]^{\otimes 2}\right),

and the asymptotic variance of
\widehat{\bm{\xi}}
in Theorem
5
is

\displaystyle{\bf\Sigma}_{\bm{\xi}}={\bf A}{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}{\bf A}^{\rm T}+{\bm{\beta}}^{\otimes 2}\hbox{var}\{v({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\},

we only need to show

\displaystyle E\left([{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)+{\bf M}{\bf X}Y-E\{{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)+{\bf M}{\bf X}Y\mid{\bf X}\}]^{\otimes 2}\right)={\bf A}{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}{\bf A}^{\rm T}.

Now we can always write
{\bm{\beta}}y^{2}+{\bf a}(y)={\bf\Lambda}{\bf B}(y)
, where
{\bf\Lambda}\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times m}
. Then (
S.5
) and (
S.6
) imply

\displaystyle-{\bf\Lambda}E[{\bf B}(y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}\mid y]+{\bm{\beta}}E\{y^{2}-E(Y^{2}\mid{\bf X})\mid y\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2{\bm{\beta}}E[yE(Y\mid{\bf X})-E\{YE(Y\mid{\bf X})\mid{\bf X}\}\mid y]+{\bf M}E[{\bf X}\{y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\mid y]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2{\bm{\beta}}E[yE(Y\mid{\bf X})-E\{YE(Y\mid{\bf X})\mid{\bf X}\}\mid y]

\displaystyle+\left(E\{v({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf I}-E\left[2{\bm{\beta}}{\bf X}^{\rm T}Yv({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})+\{{\bf\Lambda}{\bf B}(Y)-{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}\}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}{\bf X}^{\rm T}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\times{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}E[{\bf X}\{y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}\mid y].

Multiplying
{\bf B}^{\rm T}(y)
on both sides and taking expectation lead
to

\displaystyle-{\bf\Lambda}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}+{\bm{\beta}}E[\{Y^{2}-E(Y^{2}\mid{\bf X})\}{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2{\bm{\beta}}E[YE(Y\mid{\bf X}){\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)-\{E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}^{2}{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)]

\displaystyle+\left(E\{v({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf I}-E\left[2{\bm{\beta}}{\bf X}^{\rm T}Yv({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})+\{{\bf\Lambda}{\bf B}(Y)-{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}\}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf X}^{\rm T}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\times{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}E[{\bf X}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2{\bm{\beta}}E[Y\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]+E\{v({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-E\left\{2{\bm{\beta}}{\bf X}^{\rm T}Yv({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\right\}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}

\displaystyle-{\bf\Lambda}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}+{\bm{\beta}}E[Y^{2}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf X}^{\rm T}]{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2{\bm{\beta}}E[Y\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]+{\bf A}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf\Lambda}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12},

where
{\bf A}_{1},{\bf A}_{2}
are defined in Theorem
5
,
hence

\displaystyle-{\bf\Lambda}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}+{\bf\Lambda}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2{\bm{\beta}}E[Y\hbox{cov}\{Y,{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]+{\bf A}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bm{\beta}}E[\{Y^{2}-E(Y^{2}\mid{\bf X})\}{\bf B}^{\rm T}(Y)]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf A}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf A}_{2},

and

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}y^{2}+{\bf a}(y)=({\bf A}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf A}_{2})({\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf\Sigma}_{22})^{-1}{\bf B}(y)={\bf U}{\bf B}(y),

where, for notational brevity,

\displaystyle{\bf U}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle({\bf A}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf A}_{2})({\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf\Sigma}_{22})^{-1}.

Then

\displaystyle{\bf M}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(E\{v({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf I}-E\{2{\bm{\beta}}{\bf X}^{\rm T}Yv({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}-{\bf U}E\left[{\bf B}(Y)\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf X}^{\rm T}\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.+{\bm{\beta}}E\left[Y^{2}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf X}^{\rm T}\right]\right){\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(E\{v({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}{\bf I}-E\{2{\bm{\beta}}{\bf X}^{\rm T}Yv({\bm{\beta}}^{\rm T}{\bf X})\}-{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}+{\bm{\beta}}E\left[Y^{2}\{Y-E(Y\mid{\bf X})\}{\bf X}^{\rm T}\right]\right){\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf A}_{1}-{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}){\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}.

Hence

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}y^{2}+{\bf a}(y)-E\{{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)\mid{\bf x}\}={\bf U}[{\bf B}(y)-E\{{\bf B}(Y)\mid{\bf x}\}],

and

\displaystyle E\{\hbox{var}({\bf M}{\bf X}Y\mid{\bf X})\}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf M}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}{\bf M}^{\rm T},

\displaystyle E[\hbox{var}\{{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}{\bf U}^{\rm T},

\displaystyle E[\hbox{cov}\{{\bf M}{\bf X}Y,{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)\mid{\bf X}\}]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf M}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf U}^{\rm T}.

Thus, noting that
{\bf A}=[{\bf A}_{1},{\bf A}_{2}]
,

\displaystyle E\left([{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)+{\bf M}{\bf X}Y-E\{{\bm{\beta}}Y^{2}+{\bf a}(Y)+{\bf M}{\bf X}Y\mid{\bf X}\}]^{\otimes 2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf M}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}{\bf M}^{\rm T}+{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}{\bf U}^{\rm T}+{\bf M}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf U}^{\rm T}+{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf M}^{\rm T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\bf A}_{1}-{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}){\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}({\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}-{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf U}^{\rm T})+{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{22}{\bf U}^{\rm T}+({\bf A}_{1}-{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}){\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf U}^{\rm T}

\displaystyle+{\bf U}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}({\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}-{\bf\Sigma}_{12}{\bf U}^{\rm T})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf A}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}+{\bf U}({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}){\bf U}^{\rm T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf A}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}+({\bf A}_{1}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}-{\bf A}_{2})({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}({\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf A}_{1}^{\rm T}-{\bf A}_{2}^{\rm T})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf A}\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}+{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}&-{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12}({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}\\
-({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}&({\bf\Sigma}_{22}-{\bf\Sigma}_{21}{\bf\Sigma}_{11}^{-1}{\bf\Sigma}_{12})^{-1}\end{array}\right){\bf A}^{\rm T}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\bf A}{\bf\Sigma}^{-1}{\bf A}^{\rm T}.

∎
